5CCB: BASIC Program
Velnor's Lair by Derek Brewster, published by Neptune Computing in 1983. It is the first of his two "Myths and Legends of Caraland" games; Spectrum Computing also notes the Quicksilva reissue and the sequel, Kentilla.
The whole game is a ROM-loaded BASIC program. The tape header names it "VELNOR", sets autorun at line 4860, and gives a program length of 93D2 and a data length of A112. There is no custom tape loader.
Line 4860 is POKE D,F: GO TO A with the saved variables D=23728 (NMIADD), F=4 and A=1, so it writes 04 to NMIADD and starts at line 5. Line 4870 (PRINT USR TU) is not the autorun path.
Line 6035 POKEs a 10-byte routine at UDG that writes attribute 38 across the screen; line 6330 calls it with RANDOMIZE USR 65368. That code is not on the tape and is not present in this snapshot.
